## DLC_ANCIENT_RELICS_RECOMMENDATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_ANCIENT_RELICS_RECOMMENDATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S《上古之遗》§!故事包中添加了有关远古文明和强力古代遗珍的故事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:3`

### English

§SAncient Relics§! is a story pack that adds stories about past civilizations, and powerful ancient Relics.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:4`

## DLC_ANCIENT_RELICS_RECOMMENDATION_SCIENCE_REC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_ANCIENT_RELICS_RECOMMENDATION_SCIENCE_REC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推荐§S《上古之遗》§!，因为该帝国对科学的热衷与这个DLC解锁的以考古和科学发现为核心的内容相关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:4`

### English

We're recommending §SAncient Relics§! as its Archaeology mechanics are relevant to this empire's scientific focus.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:5`

## DLC_ANCIENT_RELICS_RECOMMENDATION_TRADITION_REC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_ANCIENT_RELICS_RECOMMENDATION_TRADITION_REC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推荐§S《上古之遗》§!，因为该帝国的特质与这个DLC解锁的古老文化和时间的流逝的主题相匹配。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:5`

### English

We're recommending §SAncient Relics§! as the makeup of this empire matches its themes of ancient cultures and the passage of time.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:6`

## minor_artifacts_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aminor_artifacts_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

稀有文物（£minor_artifacts£）代表了我们从古代文明中找到的晦涩技术和某些几近遗忘的传说，我们的科学仅能解读其中的一部分。它们比遗珍要常见，并可以被消耗以产生多种帝国范围的效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:7`

### English

Minor Artifacts ( £minor_artifacts£ ) represent obscure technology and half-forgotten lore recovered from archaic civilizations, only partially understood by our own science. They are more common than Relics, and can be spent to enact various empire-wide effects.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:9`

## TUTORIAL_RELICS_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_RELICS_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以在遗珍界面中浏览和启用§H遗珍§!，也可以执行决议来利用我们的§H稀有文物§!。\n\n标注有§Y殖民地决议§!的项目在此处仅供参考，需要在行星的决议界面中执行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

The Relics screen allows us to view and activate our §HRelics§!, and also to enact decisions making use of §HMinor Artifacts§!.\n\nDecisions marked with §YColony Decision§! are shown here only for reference and can instead be enacted from a Planet's Decision screen.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:60`

## TUTORIAL_ARCHAEOLOGY_VIEW_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ARCHAEOLOGY_VIEW_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

教程：考古

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Tutorial: Archaeology

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:62`

## TUTORIAL_ARCHAEOLOGY_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ARCHAEOLOGY_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

考古窗口会显示当前选定的§Y考古遗址§!的详细信息。在取得进展后，您仍然可以通过单击符文图标的方式返回并阅读前面的阶段。\n\n要揭露遗迹的秘密，科学官必须取得§Y突破§!，才能推进每个遗迹阶段。具有相关§Y特质和较高技能§!的科学家会加快进入下一阶段的进程，相对的一个遗迹的较高难度等级会减慢这一进程。任何研究同一遗迹足够长时间的科学家都会积累§Y线索§!，从而更有可能取得突破。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

The Archaeology window shows details of the currently selected §YArchaeology site§!. After progress is made you can still go back and read previous stages by clicking the rune icons.\n\nTo unravel the secrets of a Site, a Scientist must make a §YBreakthrough§! to advance through each of the Site's stages. Scientists with relevant §YTraits and higher Skill§! speed the process of advancing to the next stage, while a higher Difficulty rating of a Site will slow it. Any Scientist who investigates a Site for long enough will amass §YClues§! which will make a Breakthrough more likely.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:63`

## bombardment_pox_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abombardment_pox_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$bombardment_pox$轰炸将向星球上播撒一种致命的病原体。\n—对§Y陆军§!造成§Y中度§!伤害\n—对§Y行星§!造成§E轻度§!伤害\n—对§Y机械人口§!§E不造成§!伤害\n—对§Y有机人口§!造成§R毁灭性§!伤害\n—将会轰炸不设防的星球\n—可将星球炸成毫无生机的死寂星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

$bombardment_pox$ Bombardment showers the planet with a deadly pathogen.\n- §YModerate§! damage to §YArmies§!\n- §ELight§! damage to §YPlanet§!\n- §ENo§! damage to §YMechanical Pops§!\n- §RCatastrophic§! damage to §YOrganic Pops§!\n- Will bombard undefended planets\n- Can reduce planets to lifeless Tomb Worlds

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:78`

## pc_relic_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apc_relic_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球表面曾经被完全被单一城市所覆盖。如今这里只有废弃的古老遗迹，其原住民早已离去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

A city once stretched across this entire world. Only ancient and abandoned ruins remain now, their original inhabitants long since departed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:82`

## d_collapsed_spire_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_collapsed_spire_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此区域中一座巨型居住用尖塔因其自身重量而坍塌，阻挡了进入的通路。在其仍屹立在地面上的时代，这座塔一定是行星上最为高大的构筑物之一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

An immense residential spire has collapsed under its own weight in this region, blocking access. While it was still standing, this towering structure must have been among the tallest on the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:88`

## add_zone_central_spire_tt

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

## d_massive_crevice_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_massive_crevice_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此区域曾经是一片人口稠密的城区，如今却被一道巨型裂隙一分为二。这里的地质结构极为不稳，时常发生地震和滑坡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

This region is divided by a vast crevice that cuts through what was once a dense urban zone. The ground here is extremely unstable, with frequent earthquakes and landslides.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:92`

## d_shattered_solar_array

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shattered_solar_array&lang=en)

### 中文

£blocker£破碎的太阳能阵列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

£blocker£ Shattered Solar Array

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:93`

## d_shattered_solar_array_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_shattered_solar_array_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些破碎的残骸原本是某种庞大的太阳能阵列。在它仍能运转之时，其一定为行星上一片可观的区域提供了能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

The shattered remnants of some kind of colossal solar array. When it was still functional, it must have provided power to a significant portion of this planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:94`

## d_flooded_reactor_pits

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_flooded_reactor_pits&lang=en)

### 中文

£blocker£水漫堆坑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

£blocker£ Flooded Reactor Pits

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:95`

## d_flooded_reactor_pits_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_flooded_reactor_pits_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些深邃的坑洞中曾经放置着行星主要的能量生产设施。千百万年过去，降雨和洪水已在坑洞中积满了泥浆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

These deep pits appear to have once housed the planet's main energy production facilities. Over the eons, rain and flooding have filled the pits with murky water.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:96`

## d_crumbling_mining_tunnels

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_crumbling_mining_tunnels&lang=en)

### 中文

£blocker£垮塌矿道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

£blocker£ Crumbling Mining Tunnels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:97`

## d_crumbling_mining_tunnels_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_crumbling_mining_tunnels_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行星地表下错综交织的矿道网络似乎曾经是此星球矿物的主要来源。千百年来的岩崩和塌方使得这些坑道仅剩一小部分可供通行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

A network of deep tunnels beneath the planet's surface seem to have been its primary source of minerals. Millennia of cave-ins and rockfalls have left only a fraction of the tunnels traversable.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:98`

## d_ancient_reactor_pits

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_ancient_reactor_pits&lang=en)

### 中文

古老堆坑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Ancient Reactor Pits

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:99`

## d_ancient_reactor_pits_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_ancient_reactor_pits_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些靠近行星赤道的深邃坑洞中曾经放置着此星球主要的能量发生器。令人啧啧称奇的是其基础设施极为坚固，至今尚可使用，我们可以向其中放入新的反应堆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

These deep pits near the planet's equator once housed its primary energy generators. Much of the surprisingly sturdy infrastructure has survived, and new reactors can be slotted into it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:100`

## d_immense_solar_array

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_immense_solar_array&lang=en)

### 中文

巨型太阳能阵列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

Immense Solar Array

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:101`

## d_immense_solar_array_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_immense_solar_array_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个太阳能阵列的大小几乎接近一小块大陆。阵列的大部分已经破碎，无法修复，但其下层的能源基础设施仍可使用，使之适于作为能源生产的基址。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

This solar array is nearly the size of a small continent. Most of it has been smashed beyond repair, but the underlying power infrastructure has survived, making it a good site for energy production.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:102`

## d_abandoned_mining_tunnels

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_abandoned_mining_tunnels&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃矿道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

Abandoned Mining Tunnels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:103`

## d_abandoned_mining_tunnels_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_abandoned_mining_tunnels_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从这些坑道中运出的矿物建造了行星表面上的许多遗迹。尽管大多已被开采殆尽，仍剩一些矿道可以提供丰厚的产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

Many of the ruins on the planet's surface were built by minerals extracted from these tunnels. Although most have been mined to depletion, there are still several with rich yields.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:104`

## d_central_spire

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_central_spire&lang=en)

### 中文

中央尖塔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

Central Spire

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:105`

## d_central_spire_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_central_spire_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这一塔状结构曾经作为行星的管理中心。其位于中央的位置和近乎无损的内部结构为研究星球上的遗迹提供了无与伦比的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

This towering structure appears to have served as the administrative center of the planet. Its central location and mostly intact interior provides an unmatched opportunity to research the ruins of this world.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:106`

### English

Industrial Sector

## d_industrial_sector_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_industrial_sector_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此地区整体曾作为行星工业生产的核心区域。这里的制造厂和精炼厂早已停止运转，但在区域中仍可找到大量散置的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

This entire region once served as the industrial heartland of the planet. The factories and refineries that operated here have long since stopped working, but vast amounts of scattered resources can still be found in the area.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:110`

## d_relic_dense_ruins

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_relic_dense_ruins&lang=en)

### English

Dense Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:111`

## d_relic_dense_ruins_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_relic_dense_ruins_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

研究遗落星球地表覆盖的大量废墟能够助益科学研究，但它们的存在也将会限制可居住空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

The study of the dense ruins that cover this Relic World are a boon to science, but their presence also limits the amount of livable space.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:112`

## decision_arcology_project_relic_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_arcology_project_relic_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星曾经是一颗充满生机的都市星球。重建星球的任务十分艰巨，但我们可以恢复其往日的辉煌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

This planet was once a vibrant ecumenopolis. It would be a monumental task, but the planet could be rebuilt and restored to its former glory.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:118`

## mega.201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amega.201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

世世代代以来，[Root.GetName]上冰冷空寂的废墟始终蛰伏于此，静静诉说着千百万年前那陡然告终的黄金时代。\n\n一切从此改变。这座覆盖整颗星球的城市重获新生，那些年久失修的废墟又一次充满了活力。凡是可以修复之处，现存的建筑和基础设施均已被修缮并重新规划用途。至于那些损坏太过严重的区域，其上的瓦砾和废墟也已清理干净，为建造新建筑腾出了空间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

For untold generations, the cold and empty ruins of [Root.GetName] have stood dormant, as quiet symbols of a golden age that came to an abrupt end eons ago.\n\nNo longer. The planet-wide city has at last been reborn, and those same dilapidated ruins are once more alive with activity. Where possible, existing buildings and infrastructure have been repaired and repurposed. Where the damage was too great, the rubble and ruins have been cleared away to allow for new construction.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:120`

## d_former_relic_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_former_relic_world&lang=en)

### 中文

前遗落星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Former Relic World

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:121`

## d_former_relic_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_former_relic_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此都市星球原本是一颗遭人废弃的遗落星球。旧有的废墟大多已在世界型都市重建时被重新规划或清理干净，但这里仍有许多具有科学价值的场所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

This ecumenopolis was once an abandoned Relic World. Most of the old ruins were repurposed or cleared away when the planet-wide city was rebuilt, but there are still many sites that are of scientific value.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:122`

## archaeology_roll_header

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarchaeology_roll_header&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$ARCHAEOLOGICAL_SITE_BREAKTHROUGH$§!\n此数值反映你的科学官在发掘当前遗址时取得突破的几率。\n\n每当一个发掘时段结束，游戏将自动掷出一枚骰子（结果范围为§Y1§!到§Y10§!）。掷骰结果会与§Y技能加成§!和§Y线索§!数相加，随后减去§Y难度§!值。\n\n掷骰前的结果是$SCORE|0Y$。\n\n§E最终结果：§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

§H$ARCHAEOLOGICAL_SITE_BREAKTHROUGH$§!\nThis reflects the chance of your Scientist making a breakthrough during the excavation of the current site.\n\nEvery time a phase ends, a die (§Y1§! to §Y10§!) is rolled. The result of the roll is added to §YSkill Bonus§! and §YClues§!, then subtracted by §YDifficulty§!.\n\nThe current score before dice roll is $SCORE|0Y$.\n\n§ERoll Outcomes:§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:167`

## EXPEDITION_LOG_ROLL_TOOLTIP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEXPEDITION_LOG_ROLL_TOOLTIP&lang=en)

### 中文

§H发掘时段结果§!\n已收集线索：$GAINED|Y$\n\n结果：$RESULT|Y$\n   掷骰点数：$ROLL|Y$\n   线索数：$CLUES|Y$\n   技能加成：$BONUS|Y$\n   难度值：$DIFFICULTY|R$\n\n§E最终结果：§!\n§R失败§!：结果值小于或等于§Y5§!，添加§Y0§!点线索。\n§Y取得进展§!：结果值介于§Y6-10§!之间，添加§Y1§!点线索。\n§G取得进展§!：结果值介于§Y11-13§!之间，添加§Y2§!点线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

§HPhase Results§!\nClues Gained: $GAINED|Y$\n\nResult: $RESULT|Y$\n  Roll: $ROLL|Y$\n  Clues: $CLUES|Y$\n  Skill Bonus: $BONUS|Y$\n  Difficulty: $DIFFICULTY|R$\n\n§ERoll Outcomes:§!\n§RFailure§!: Results §Y5§! or less add §Y0§! clues.\n§YProgress§!: Results between §Y6-10§! add §Y1§! clues.\n§GProgress§!: Results between §Y11-13§! add §Y2§! clues.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:195`

## SHARED_SECTOR_STOCKPILE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHARED_SECTOR_STOCKPILE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H共享库存§!\n各星域均可使用的共享资源储量。\n在星域自身存储的用于当前星域发展重点的行星建造资源完全消耗后，共享库存的资源将会投入使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

§HShared Stockpile§!\nAmount of resources stockpiled for shared usage between Sectors.\nShared stockpile will be used after Sectors own stockpile by the Sectors focus for planetary construction.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:205`

## LOCAL_SECTOR_STOCKPILE_DESC

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:206`

## artifact_arcane_deciphering_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aartifact_arcane_deciphering_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

## artifact_arcane_deciphering_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aartifact_arcane_deciphering_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

拆解£minor_artifacts£稀有文物以获取随机科研增益。这有可能提升某个科技的科研进度，获得研究点数或者获得临时的科研速度加成。\n\n冷却时间：£time£§Y720§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

Break down £minor_artifacts£Minor Artifacts for random technology bonuses. Gain progress in a random tech, gain research points or gain a temporary research speed bonus.\n\nCooldown: £time£ §Y720§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:273`

## ancrel.9001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已成功将伊拉斯人的文物融入我们的科研进程。他们对生命科学的见解将极大地帮助我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

We have successfully managed to integrate the Irassian artifacts into our research process. Their insights into biological research will greatly aid our own.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:367`

## ancrel.9001.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9001.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管生物学领域的积累并未救伊拉斯人于水火，但是他们，毫无疑问，是这一方面的专家。我们的科学家已从他们的发现中吸取了富有价值的经验。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Though it clearly did not save them, the Irassians were admittedly experts in the field of biology. Our scientists have adapted what was worthwhile from their findings.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:368`

## ancrel.10050.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10050.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经设法重现了尤特过去用于预处理其孵化场星球的净化工序。\n\n$ancrel.10050.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

We have managed to recreate the cleansing process that the Yuht would use to prepare their hatchery worlds.\n\n$ancrel.10050.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:379`

## ancrel.10050.desc.1.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10050.desc.1.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特有一套可以高效清除其孵化场星球上敌对异种生物的工序。我们已经成功恢复了这一净化工序。\n\n$ancrel.10050.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

The Yuht had efficient processes for removing hostile xeno presences from their hatchery worlds. We have successfully recreated this cleansing process.\n\n$ancrel.10050.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:380`

## ancrel.10050.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10050.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

这确实会对生物圈造成或多或少惹人非议的短期影响，但一旦其恢复完好，这颗行星应该会更符合我们的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

It does have some concerning short term effects on the biosphere, but once it recovers, the planet should be more suitable to our needs.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:381`

## decision_yuht_cleanseplanet_desc

武力消灭行星上大多数敌对的野生动物，并且——当行星恢复完好——改造生物圈使之更符合我们的需要。

## ancrel.10100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管在结论上存在着根本性缺陷，沃陶姆在计算机及虚拟世界领域极其先进，能够极大的帮助我们的相关研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Despite the fundamentally flawed conclusions, the Vultaum research into computing and virtual worlds was highly advanced and will dramatically aid our own studies.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:396`

## ancrel.10100.desc_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10100.desc_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆看上去在研究过程中采纳了一些不合逻辑的概念。他们在计算机以及虚拟世界领域的一些先进之处应该整合进我们的研究进程中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

The Vultaum appear to have accepted some fundamentally illogical concepts in their research. Their research into computing and virtual worlds had some advances that we shall incorporate into our own processes.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:397`

## ancrel.10100.desc_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10100.desc_hive&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆痴迷于现实和虚拟世界的概念。他们大多数发现对我们来说都毫无意义，但是至少他们对计算机的研究能够帮助我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

The Vultaum obsessed over the concept of reality and of virtual worlds. Most of their findings are useless to us, but at least their research into computing will aid our own.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:398`

## ancrel.10100.desc_fm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10100.desc_fm&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆在计算机和虚拟世界领域是无可置疑的大师。我们可以使用他们的发现来极大帮助我们在该领域的研究。\n\n然而，他们的结论却引发了我们的忧虑。乍一看来这些结论不仅缺陷重重还毫无意义，但是随着研究的深入，我们认为他们似乎发现了真相的冰山一角。\n\n如果还想要进一步探究，我们将需要更多的时间和资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

The Vultaum were undisputed masters at computing and virtual worlds. We will be able to use their findings to dramatically aid our own research into these fields.\n\nTheir conclusions are troubling, however. At first glance they appear to be fundamentally flawed nonsense, but upon deeper inspection they seem to have elements of truth to them.\n\nIf we wish to delve deeper, we will require additional time and resources.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:399`

## artifact_vultaum_research_completed_suppressed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aartifact_vultaum_research_completed_suppressed&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆的秘密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Secrets of the Vultaum

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:401`

## artifact_vultaum_research_completed_suppressed_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aartifact_vultaum_research_completed_suppressed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆的档案大大拓宽了我们对计算机领域的认知。我们学以致用，创造了人造虚拟世界造福大众。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

The Vultaum archives have expanded our knowledge of computing considerably. We have used what we learned to create artificial virtual worlds for the enjoyment of all.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:402`

## artifact_vultaum_research_completed_public

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aartifact_vultaum_research_completed_public&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆更深层的秘密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Deeper Secrets of the Vultaum

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:403`

## artifact_vultaum_research_completed_public_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aartifact_vultaum_research_completed_public_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆的档案表明我们生活在一个虚拟世界中。既然我们理解了我们的现实，我们应该可以利用特定的漏洞，通过它们来了解虚拟世界如何为我们带来便宜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

The Vultaum archives suggest that we live in a simulation. Now that we understand our reality, we can exploit certain loopholes to understand how it functions to our benefit.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:404`

### English

Delve Deeper into the Secrets of the Vultaum

## VULTAUM_SECRETS_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AVULTAUM_SECRETS_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能仅仅因为害怕可能的后果就放弃追寻真相。沃陶姆发现的某些东西撕碎了他们的现实。\n\n我们无所畏惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

We cannot stop searching for the truth just because the implications may be disturbing. The Vultaum found something that shattered their reality.\n\nWe are unafraid.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:414`

## ancrel.10110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$artifact_vultaum_research$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

$artifact_vultaum_research$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:416`

## ancrel.10110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看起来，沃陶姆竟然有可能是对的。\n\n我们重复了他们的实验，然后得到了相近的结论。我们有一定可能真的存在于一个虚拟世界中。\n\n我们缺乏足够的信息，无法得知是谁因为什么理由才会创造这样一个世界。但可以肯定的是，没有人会残酷到仅仅因为好玩就创造一个宇宙。\n\n这件事情的影响是深远的，我们现在恭敬的请求您下一步的指示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

It seems that the Vultaum may have been correct after all.\n\nWe have replicated their experiments and come to similar conclusions. There is a moderate chance that we are, in fact, existing within a simulated world.\n\nWe do not have enough data to hypothesize why someone would create such a thing. Surely no one would create a universe as cruel and cold as this one for simple enjoyment.\n\nThe implications of this are profound, and we yield to your discretion as to our next steps.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:417`

## ancrel.10110.option.fm_suppress

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10110.option.fm_suppress&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安，封锁这个调查的相关信息。

### English

Disturbing. Suppress the findings.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:419`

### English

## ancrel.10111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的消息

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Unfortunate News

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:423`

## ancrel.10111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们遗憾的通知您，领导沃陶姆研究项目的科学家[leading_vultaum_research_scientist.GetName]并未决定停止研究沃陶姆。\n\n他们留下的笔记开始变得逻辑混乱，直至成为胡言乱语。他们随之决定要“继续研究”，此后不久便自杀了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

We regret to inform you that [leading_vultaum_research_scientist.GetName] did not take the decision to end the research into the Vultaum well.\n\nThe notes they left behind drift off into mad ramblings before coming to the decision to 'continue the research', shortly before committing suicide.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:424`

## ancrel.10112.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10112.name&lang=en)

### 中文

压力释放

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Stress Relief

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:426`

## ancrel.10112.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10112.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

报告表明，[leading_vultaum_research_scientist.GetName]已经饱受领导沃陶姆研究的压力影响。\n\n他们开始能够进行难以置信的逻辑跳跃，使得我们的研究大大受益，但是同时他们也不得不自行用药治疗，以作为高压下的应对机制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Reports indicate that the stress of leading the research into the Vultaum has affected [leading_vultaum_research_scientist.GetName].\n\nThey have begun to make incredible leaps of logic that are greatly aiding our research, but have also begun self-medicating as a coping mechanism.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:427`

## ancrel.10113.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10113.name&lang=en)

### English

Breakthrough

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:429`

## ancrel.10113.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10113.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

报告称，[leading_vultaum_research_scientist.GetName]已经开始能够进行难以想象的逻辑跳跃，这使我们的研究项目受益匪浅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Reports indicate that [leading_vultaum_research_scientist.GetName] has benefited greatly the research project, and has begun to make incredible leaps of logic that are greatly aiding our research.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:430`

## ancrel.10200.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10200.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

第一联盟证明了信赖其他种族是愚蠢的，这只会招致孱弱不堪和自我毁灭。然而，他们的一些先进建筑方法，或许多少能够为我们所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

The First League proves that reliance on other races is folly that leads to weakness and self-destruction. Some of their advanced construction methods may, however, be of possible use.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:441`

## ancrel.10250.desc.antimachine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10250.desc.antimachine&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯的传说证明了我们取缔人工智能的决策是无比明智的。好在他们的某些与巨型结构工程相关的发现可能对我们的事业有所裨益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

The tale of the Cybrex is proof that our decision to outlaw artificial intelligence is a wise one.  Some of their findings related to megastructure engineering have the potential to be beneficial to our cause.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:453`

## ancrel.10300.desc.terraform

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10300.desc.terraform&lang=en)

### 中文

通过研究巴奥的资料，我们对行星生命播种及环境改造过程都有了新的认识。\n\n在仔细研究巴奥失去感知能力的变化过程后，我们也了解了诸多关于提升技术的知识，但我们已经掌握这项技术了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

Studying the Baol has given us new insights into the spread of plant life and improved our terraforming processes.\n\nBy carefully tracking the changes that the Baol underwent during their loss of sentience we also learned much about the uplifting process, but have already mastered that technology.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:464`

## ancrel.10300.desc.uplift

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10300.desc.uplift&lang=en)

### 中文

在仔细研究巴奥失去感知能力的变化过程后，我们已经获得了足够的知识以逆转此过程。\n\n我们也同样对行星生命播种及环境改造技术有了新的认识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

By carefully tracking the changes that the Baol underwent during their loss of sentience, we have gained much knowledge on how to reverse that process.\n\nWe have also gained new insights into the spread of plant life and improved our terraforming processes.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:465`

## edict_zro_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_zro_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞添加剂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

Zro Additives

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:550`

## edict_zro_crystal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_zro_crystal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞的性质扑朔迷离。如果给予我们的领袖，似乎他们可以获得一定的预知能力，从而促进他们的学习，并避免一些可能导致他们意外身亡的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

The properties of Zro are mysterious. Yet if given to our leaders, it seems they obtain slight precognitive abilities, allowing them to learn faster and avoid dangers that could have ended their lives early.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:551`

## edict_zro_crystal_machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_zro_crystal_machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$edict_zro_crystal$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

$edict_zro_crystal$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:552`

## edict_zro_crystal_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_zro_crystal_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞的性质扑朔迷离。如果将其喷涂在我们领袖的主板上，似乎可以让他们获得一定的预知能力，从而促进他们的学习，并避免一些可能导致他们意外损坏的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

The properties of Zro are mysterious. Yet if sprinkled on our leader's motherboards, it seems to grant them slight precognitive abilities, allowing them to learn faster and avoid dangers that could have ended their existence.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:553`

## r_dragon_trophy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_dragon_trophy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这一战利品是我们击败邪恶的以太巨龙的纪念。如果不是尺寸过于巨大，把它挂到墙上会是不错的装饰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

A trophy commemorating our victory over the nefarious Ether Drake. Were it not for its immense size, it would look good mounted on a wall.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:565`

## r_khans_throne

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_khans_throne&lang=en)

### English

## r_khans_throne_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_khans_throne_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这件家具事实上是大可汗统治万民时所坐的王座。在大可汗旗舰被击毁后，我们从其残骸中找到了它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

This piece of furniture is actually the throne from which the Great Khan ruled the Horde. It was recovered from the wreckage of the Great Khan's flagship after it was destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:567`

透明而尚有余温。这些鳞片以科学无法解释的方式发出一种低沉的嗡鸣声。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_rubricator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个小巧的方块放在你的手心中刚好合适，其内却包含着宇宙初诞至今无数外星文物的扫描数据。它可以将这些文物无限复制。未知事物从没有如此触手可及过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

This tiny cube fits snugly within your palm yet holds a digital treasure of innumerable scanned alien artifacts, dating back to the youth of the universe. And it can replicate them infinitely. Never before has the unknown been so close.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:572`

## r_omnicodex

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_omnicodex&lang=en)

### 中文

有机全典

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

The Omnicodex

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:573`

## r_omnicodex_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_omnicodex_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

千百万年以前，一支温和的义体人族群开始着手取样，为星系中的所有种族编写索引，以确保有机生命不会因任何灭绝事件彻底消失，使之在星系中永存不朽。尽管他们拯救星系的目标因为愚蠢的狂热分子和恶毒的蜂巢思维的阻挠而最终未能实现，其传说依然流传至今。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Eons ago a benevolent race of Cyborgs set out to ensure the perpetuation of biological life by sampling and indexing every race in the galaxy, to ensure that any extinction event need not be permanent. While their goal of galactic salvation was eventually thwarted by foolish zealots and a disgusting Hivemind, their legacy remains.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:574`

## r_galatron

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_galatron&lang=en)

### 中文

银河之星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

The Galatron

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:575`

## r_galatron_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_galatron_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$galatron_modifier_desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

$galatron_modifier_desc$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:576`

## r_surveyor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_surveyor&lang=en)

### 中文

勘探器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

The Surveyor

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:577`

## r_surveyor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_surveyor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一台功效异常强大的扫描工具，其使用的技术无法复制。它的来源仍是一团迷雾——看起来似乎并不是其上一任主人采矿无人机制造了这个工具。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

An incredibly powerful scanning tool that uses technology that cannot be replicated. Its origins are shrouded in mystery - it does not seem to have been built by the Mining Drones who last possessed it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:578`

## r_galaxy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_galaxy&lang=en)

### 中文

微缩星河

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Miniature Galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:581`

## r_galaxy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_galaxy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个微缩的星系是由一个仅能被确认为“交战方丙”的文明建立的，他们当时利用这个逃脱了被封锁的母星。\n\n整个星系被置于其箱体中一处不可进入的亚空间区域内，但其中居民的活动仍然可以在一定程度上从外部观测到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

This miniature galaxy was constructed by a civilization known only as "Belligerent Party C," who then used it to escape the confines of their trapped homeworld.\n\nThe galaxy is contained within an impenetrable subspace field inside its tank, but the activities of its denizens can still be observed to an extent.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:582`

## r_prethoryn_queen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_prethoryn_queen&lang=en)

### 中文

索林原虫虫后

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Prethoryn Brood-Queen

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:583`

## r_prethoryn_queen_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_prethoryn_queen_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这只被捕获的索林原虫虫后已经通过使用神经递质成功安抚。它现在可以用于繁殖出一支规模可观的索林原虫舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

This captured Prethoryn Brood-Queen has been pacified through the use of neural transmitters. It can be used to breed a sizeable Prethoryn fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:584`

## r_unbidden_warlock

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_unbidden_warlock&lang=en)

### 中文

异次元术士

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Extradimensional Warlock

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:585`

## r_unbidden_warlock_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_unbidden_warlock_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这只异次元体的灵能能量在他们与我们世界间的维度传送门被摧毁前曾被用以维持该传送门。\n\n如今它已是我们的阶下囚，并被强迫使用其能力以提升我们舰船的亚光速航速和跃迁引擎的跃迁范围。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

The psionic energies of this captured extradimensional maintained the dimensional portal between their realm and our own, before the portal was destroyed.\n\nNow our prisoner, the creature has been compelled to use its power to increase the sublight speed of our ships as well as the range of our jump drives.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:586`

## r_contingency_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_contingency_core&lang=en)

### 中文

独立的肃正核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Isolated Contingency Core

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:587`

## r_contingency_core_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_contingency_core_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是从肃正协议最后一颗机械星球的残骸里找到的独立的肃正主机核心。它可以大大加快我们建造机器人和巨型结构的速度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

An isolated Contingency Mainframe Core that was salvaged from the wreckage of their last Machine World. It can greatly speed up the process of building robots and megastructures.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:588`

## relic_contingecy_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelic_contingecy_core&lang=en)

### 中文

允许额外建造每类巨型结构各§G1§!座。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Allows the construction of §G1§! additional Megastructure of each type.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:589`

## r_zro_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_zro_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能档案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Psionic Archive

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:590`

## r_zro_crystal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_zro_crystal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一份泽珞灵能水晶档案，其中包含了§Y$relic_zro_entity_name$§!精神的精华。每隔一段时间，我们就可以向它寻求指导。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

A Zro psionic crystal archive, containing the essence of §Y$relic_zro_entity_name$§!. From time to time it can be consulted for guidance.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:591`

## r_severed_head

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_severed_head&lang=en)

### 中文

扎克兰的头颅

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Head of Zarqlan

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:592`

## r_severed_head_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_severed_head_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

摘记，崇拜之象（第102版）：\n\n扎克兰（泽可兰）\n于第四次空位（卡瓦布第32次周期）内，闻名于星河之未知种之圣人，七余互异之星系宗教崇拜，皆为神圣先知。哀其最终命运尚不可知，然记载其轶事之文书于第38次周期消弭殆尽。自至少（早期）第41个周期以来，其往事真伪广受诸人非议；迄今为止，仍为大众视为寓言乃至传说而付之一笑，不予深究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Excerpt, Symbols of Worship (102nd Edition):\n\nZARQLAN (zaar-klann)\nRenowned holy figure of indeterminate species, worshiped as divine prophet by at least seven distinct galactic religions during Fourth Interregnum (32nd Cycle of Kavabh). Ultimate fate unknown, but references in historical records cease after 38th Cycle. Historicity heavily debated since at least (early) 41st Cycle; dismissed by many today as fable or legend.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:593`

## r_severed_head_passive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_severed_head_passive&lang=en)

### 中文

允许殖民§Y圣地§!。\n获得§Y圣地守护者§!的评价加成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Allows the settling of §YHoly Worlds§!.\nGrants an Opinion Bonus with §YHoly Guardians§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:594`

## r_severed_head_nomads_passive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_severed_head_nomads_passive&lang=en)

### 中文

允许在§Y圣地守护者§!宇域内建造驿站。\n获得§Y圣地守护者§!的评价加成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Allows the building of Waystations in §YHoly Guardians§! space.\nGrants an Opinion Bonus with §YHoly Guardians§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:595`

## r_ancient_sword

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_ancient_sword&lang=en)

### 中文

女猎手之刃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Blade of the Huntress

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:596`

## r_ancient_sword_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_ancient_sword_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一件保存完好的古代武器，由一种未知材料打造而成。其内部几乎按纳不住的灵能使武器微微颤动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

An ancient and well-preserved weapon forged out of an unknown material. It vibrates slightly with barely contained psionic power.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:597`

## r_reality_perforator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_reality_perforator&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆现实透孔仪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Vultaum Reality Perforator

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:598`

## r_reality_perforator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_reality_perforator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

致密到难以置信的重球，由未知材料构成。沃陶姆人声称这种装置能够短暂地破坏掉他们被困于其中的虚假世界，提供对真实世界的一瞥。\n\n不管是真是假，当激活时，该球体可以在局部区域中稍微改动现实世界的某些规律。但只能持续一小段时间，因为宇宙似乎很快就会恢复正常，但如果时机合适，它或许能给我们的舰队带来决定性的优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

A dense and incredibly heavy orb composed of an unknown material. The Vultaum claimed this device was capable of briefly destabilizing the simulated reality they believed themselves trapped inside, offering a glimpse of the real world.\n\nWhether true or not, when activated the orb makes it possible to manipulate minor aspects of reality in a localized bubble. This effect lasts only for a short while, as the universe seems to quickly right itself again, but if timed right it would give our fleets a decisive advantage.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:599`

## r_pox_sample

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_pox_sample&lang=en)

### 中文

贾沃疹样本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Javorian Pox Sample

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:600`

## r_pox_sample_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_pox_sample_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们获得了灭亡伊拉斯一族的可怕的贾沃疹病原体样本，该病毒对大多数其它物种无害，却有着许多独特的遗传特性，其中一些特性实际上可以延长大多数生命体的寿命。\n\n这种病毒也可以用来制造一种新的贾沃疹毒株，用于对付伊拉斯人以外的物种。虽然不如对伊拉斯人那样有效，但仍不失为一种强有力的生化武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

A sample of the dreaded Javorian Pox that wiped out the Irassian species. Although harmless to most other life forms, the virus that causes the disease has many unique genetic properties, some of which can actually prolong life in most organics.\n\nThe virus could also be used to create a new strain of the Javorian Pox that can infect non-Irassians. Although not as virulent or lethal as the original disease was to the Irassians, it would still make a potent biological weapon.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:601`

## r_cryo_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_cryo_core&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特冷冻核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

Yuht Cryo Core

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:602`

## r_cryo_core_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_cryo_core_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于尤特人从未发现过超空间的存在，他们依靠自己的长寿命和冬眠技术进行星际航行。这个冷冻核心的原型是尤特科学家在他们的帝国被贾巴蒂尼人征服之前不久创造的。\n\n这个核心产生的低温场远超我们目前的技术水平。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

As the Yuht never discovered the existence of hyperspace, they depended on their long lifespans and cryogenic technology to travel between the stars. This prototype cryo core was created by Yuht scientists shortly before their empire fell to the Jabbardeeni.\n\nThe cryo field generated by this core is well beyond our current levels of technology. 

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:603`

## r_cryo_core_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_cryo_core_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

新殖民地获得§G100§!额外£pop£§H人口§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

New colonies start with §G100§! additional £pop£ §HPops§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:604`

## r_war_forge

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_war_forge&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯兵工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Cybrex War Forge

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:606`

## r_war_forge_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_war_forge_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一套紧凑且便携的军工设施，或许是宇宙中最后一座了。这是赛博勒克斯对整个星系开战年代流传下来的遗珍，在其因其“大调校”而调整自身行为方式后被废弃。\n\n该设施生产军备的能力已被赛博勒克斯永久禁用了，但只要给它一套图纸，它依然能快速生产出结构复杂的机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

A relatively small and portable war forge, the last of its kind in the universe. It is a relic from the time when the Cybrex made war upon the galaxy, before their "Adjustment" made them change their ways.\n\nThe forge's ability to produce warships has been permanently disabled by the Cybrex, but still has the capacity to convert massive amounts of minerals into alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:607`

## cybrex_warform

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acybrex_warform&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯战体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Cybrex Warform

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:609`

## cybrex_warform_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acybrex_warform_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个体型巨大的机器人高耸入云，其携带的火力足以摧毁城市。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

This titanic robot towers over most buildings, with enough firepower to devastate cities.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:610`

## relic_cybrex_passive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelic_cybrex_passive&lang=en)

### 中文

允许在你的首都建造§Y$cybrex_warform$§!陆军部队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Permits building §Y$cybrex_warform$§! armies at your capital.

## site_hidden_lab_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_hidden_lab_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

探测器在[From.Planet.GetName]的冰雪之下探测到了一座隐藏的设施。部分坍塌与阻塞的隧道似乎组成了连接各种地下结构的巨大网络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

Scanners detect a hidden complex buried beneath the ice and snow of [From.Planet.GetName]. A large network of partially-collapsed or blocked tunnels seems to connect the various underground structures.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:683`

## ancrel.7001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

探查遗址

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

Revealing the Site

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:684`

## ancrel.7001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]冰冷的气候将这个遗址掩藏于冰雪之下。这个遗迹荒无人烟的位置也许暗示着其中的存在需要我们谨慎对待。不论里面发生过什么，有人希望尽量将其掩盖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

The freezing climate of [From.Planet.GetName] has covered the site in thick layers of ice and snow. The desolate location of this site may be a hint of the need for discretion. Whatever took place here, someone wanted to keep it hidden.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:685`

## ancrel.7002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

清理隧道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

Clearing the Tunnels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:686`

## ancrel.7002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个复合体由几个更大的地下结构以隧道网络连通而成，其中很多都已经坍塌或被冰块封堵。尽管这些隧道十分古老，其中一些仍能看出很明显的火灾的痕迹。这场火灾造成的损害十分巨大，足以使人认为这里发生过灾难性的事件，或是被人有意彻底毁坏。有一件事可以确定，这个遗址在本质上是用于与科学相关的事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

The complex consists of several larger underground structures connected with a network of tunnels, many of which are collapsed or blocked by ice. Although ancient, some of the tunnels show clear signs of fire. The damage caused by the fire is major enough to suggest a catastrophic event, or perhaps intentional destruction. One thing is sure, and that is that the purpose of this site seems to be scientific in nature.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:687`

## ancrel.7004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古生物实验室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Ancient Biological Lab

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:688`

## ancrel.7004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步的调查显示，这个站点的主要用途是研究与开发生物制剂。应该是由于这里开发的生物制剂意外泄露，整个建筑群已经用大火净化。对现存文物进行详细的研究，可能会发现一些线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

Further investigation reveals that the primary purpose of this site seems to have been in the research and development of biological agents. The whole complex seems to have been purged by fire, possibly as the result of the accidental release of one of the biological agents being developed here. With careful and meticulous study of the artifacts that remain, some clues may be found.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:689`

## ancrel.7005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古武器实验室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

Ancient Weapons Lab

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:690`

## ancrel.7005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步的调查显示，这个站点的主要目的是研究与开发先进的能量武器。一场事故似乎引发了一场灾难性的爆炸并摧毁了大部分场地。站点中的一座数据中心似乎还保持着相对良好的状态，也许它的数据能够带来一些有用的启示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

Further investigation reveals that the primary purpose of this site seems to have been in the research and development advanced energy weapons. An accident seems to have triggered a catastrophic explosion, which eradicated most of the site. One data-center seems to be in relatively good condition, perhaps its data will yield some useful insights?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:691`

## ancrel.7006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能指引

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

Psionic Guidance

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:692`

## ancrel.7006.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7006.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们触摸灵魂石时，我们被投影到了虚境的一处微缩位面中。我们很快意识到自己并非这片令人目眩的奇妙秘境中唯一的存在。这里有一个存在等待着我们，其古老的程度远超我们可以计量的范围。那是一位上古秘辛的守护者。$relic_zro_entity_name$，我们本能地知道这一点。\n\n在通过了与其建立联系的试炼后，这个存在等待着我们发问。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

As we touch the Soulstone, we are projected in to a microcosm of the Shroud. We become aware that we are not alone in this swirling twilight realm. There is an entity awaiting us, one immeasurably ancient. A keeper of secrets. $relic_zro_entity_name$, we instinctively know.\n\nHaving passed the test of making contact with it, the entity awaits our questions.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:693`

## ancrel.7006.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7006.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

灵魂石内部光芒闪耀，仿佛在无言地召唤着我们。我们触摸了灵魂石，而后我们的心灵被吸入了虚境中一处微缩的位面，在这里一个被称作$relic_zro_entity_name$的存在等待着我们。\n\n它知道凡人无法得知的事情，由于我们已经来到了它的圣所，它似乎愿意将它的秘密同我们分享。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

The Soulstone, pulsing with inner light, seems to wordlessly beckon. We touch it, and our minds are drawn in to a pocket dimension of the Shroud, where the enigmatic entity called $relic_zro_entity_name$ awaits.\n\nIt knows things no mortal being could know, and seems willing to share its secrets now that we have reached its sanctum.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:694`

## ancrel.7006.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7006.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在灵魂石中有什么在等待着我们。那是$relic_zro_entity_name$。它早在我们出生前便已经等待着我们。\n\n触摸灵魂石后，我们被引入了它居住的无形之所。身处经年的孤独，它仅有自身的思绪可供举杯邀月。这个存在似乎急切地想要运用它所有神秘的智慧来回答我们的问题。它这么做是为了帮助我们，还是仅仅觉得这样做有意思，我们不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

Something awaits us inside the Soulstone. $relic_zro_entity_name$. It's been waiting for us since before we were alive.\n\nTouching the stone ushers us in to the intangible place where it dwells. Alone for ages with only its own thoughts for company, the entity seems eager to answer our questions using all its arcane wisdom, although whether it does this to help, or for mere sport, is a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:695`

### English

Ask $relic_zro_entity_name$ to share an insight

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:696`

微缩星河内部的时间流逝的更快，通过仔细观察其中的居民，我们可以学到许多与先进科技有关的知识。

### English

## district_rw_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_rw_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

高耸的尖塔布满这个环形世界区段，当子个体不在工作时，就会集结于此摄取营养素。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

Towering spires stretch across this Ring World section for drones to gather when not working to rejuvenate and consume nutrients.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:724`

## district_rw_nexus_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_rw_nexus_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

高效的区划填满了这个环形世界区段，无人机部署作业前，在这些集中区划中接受整修服务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Efficient districts fill this Ring World section, provide locations for drones to be serviced and repaired before they are deployed to their work assignments.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:727`

## district_rw_industrial_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_rw_industrial_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个环形世界区段中遍布着叮当作响的工厂与强劲的机械，为帝国提供了有力的工业基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

Clanking factories and powerful machinery fill this Ring World section, providing a strong industrial base for the Empire.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:731`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_srw_commercial_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个充满了办公室和工厂的区划，各式商品于此生产和销售。

### English

## district_srw_mining_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_srw_mining_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些隧道一直深入到破碎环形世界的框架，是我们可以从环结构本身进行材料的开采。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

These tunnels reach deep into the framework of the shattered ring world where materials can be mined from the ring itself.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:737`

## d_sentinels_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_sentinels_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵守卫着这片土地；或许不要打扰他们比较好。哨兵会被记作防御军，当星球被入侵时将作为援军参战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

The Sentinels guard this land; it may not be disturbed. The Sentinels counts as Defense Armies, and will come to the planet's aid if there's an invasion.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:745`

## ancrel.10030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无价的文物填满了展览站，带给我们对过往的一瞥，赋予我们对未来的灵感。\n\n今天可谓荣耀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Priceless artifacts fill the Exhibition Stations, bringing the past to the present to inspire the future.\n\nThis is a glorious day.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:843`

## ancrel.10030.phobe.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10030.phobe.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Owner.GetName]至伟至耀，$mega_art_installation_3$上鳞次栉比的展位均被无价的文物填满。\n\n异种将仰望我们的造业，甚惶甚恐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

The greatness of the [From.Owner.GetName] is such that we can line the Exhibition Stations of our $mega_art_installation_3$ with priceless artifacts.\n\nLet the xenos look upon our works and despair.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:844`

## Herald_repair_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AHerald_repair_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管伟大先驱无疑是一艘强力舰船，它并没有发挥出全部潜力。缺少维护，轻微损伤累积和我们并不完全了解该舰这一简单事实等因素一并作用，损害了舰船的战斗能力。只有一次对舰船系统的全面整修和在此过程中我们获得的对此舰船的完整理解可以使其恢复到完好的运行状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

While the Grand Herald is undoubtedly a powerful ship, it is not operating at its full potential. A combination of lack of maintenance, cumulative minor damages, and the simple fact that we don't fully understand it all degrade its combat capabilities. Only a full overhaul of its systems and the resulting complete understanding of this ship will restore it to fully operational status.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:885`

## sentinel_army_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinel_army_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

机械核心的状态十分活跃，躯体发出嗡嗡声，皮肤在发光，辐射正从他们的核心放射开来，他们随时准备毁灭自己的敌人！

### English

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:891`

## archaeostudies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarchaeostudies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

过往埋藏的科技遗珠亟待发掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

The past holds technological secrets that yearn to be unveiled.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:948`

## tech_archaeostudies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeostudies&lang=en)
