远古学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

Archaeostudies

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:949`

## tech_archaeostudies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeostudies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该领域的研究致力于开发远古文物的可行性应用。我们远不是星系里第一批涉足繁星的文明。只要加以努力，他们的遗产便能照亮我们的前程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

This new field of studies focuses on the practical applications of ancient artifacts. We are far from being the first galactic civilization to tread these stars. With a bit of work, their legacies could secure our future.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:950`

## tech_secrets_cybrex

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_cybrex&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯采矿核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

Cybrex Mining Hub

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:952`

## tech_secrets_cybrex_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_cybrex_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯在建造环形世界方面依赖一种可以调查和收获轨道资源的高效采矿无人机。赛博勒克斯制造的存储芯片和传感器可以增强我们自己的采矿作业，以此我们得以高效利用他们远古的算法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

To build their ring world, the Cybrex relied on highly efficient mining drones to survey and harvest orbital resources. Cybrex-made memory chips and sensors could augment our own mining operations, so that we might put their millennia old algorithms to good use.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:953`

## tech_secrets_league

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_league&lang=en)

### 中文

第一联盟归档办公室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

First League Filing Offices

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:954`

## tech_secrets_league_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_league_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巅峰时期的第一联盟其庞大的帝国规模让我们相形见绌，唯有无可匹敌的官僚体系才能让这台巨大的机械顺畅运转。他们所使用的并行归档阵列在归类和存储文件方面提供了前所未有的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

The sprawling empire of the First League dwarfed our own at its peak, and relied on implacable bureaucracy to keep the wheels of the state in motion. The quantic filing arrays they used afforded them unprecedented efficiency in the sorting and storing of documents.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:955`

## tech_secrets_irassian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_irassian&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉斯人海军船坞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

Irassian Naval Yards

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:956`

## tech_secrets_irassian_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_irassian_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉斯人的太空海军传统在其时代可谓无与伦比。海军舰船和商船如同流水般从其船坞中汩汩涌出，这可能也招致了他们自身的衰弱。由于伊拉斯人制造了如此海量的舰船并又对其极度依赖，他们完全无力控制疫情最终毁灭了整个帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

The Irassian astronaval tradition was unmatched in their time. Their shipyards produced a constant stream of ships for their navy and merchant fleets, which would ultimately be their downfall. With so many ships, and such a high reliance on them, the Irassians were powerless to quarantine the disease that would claim their empire.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:957`

## tech_secrets_vultaum

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_vultaum&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆现实计算机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

Vultaum Reality Computer

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:958`

## tech_secrets_vultaum_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_vultaum_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆人的现实计算机是一种独特的、专用于分析“存在”本身的处理单元，其工作并不通过物理，而更多像是在对现实的源代码进行逆向工程，并寻找其中的漏洞以让沃陶姆人利用来拆解他们认为自己所生活在其中的这个“模拟过程”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

The reality computers of the Vultaums were unique processing units, dedicated to analyzing existence itself, not quite through physics, but rather by trying to reverse engineer reality's source code and find the glitch that would finally allow the Vultaums to take down the simulation where they believed they lived.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:959`

## tech_secrets_yuht

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_yuht&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特天文干涉仪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

Yuht Astronomical Interferometer

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:960`

## tech_secrets_yuht_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_yuht_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特文明是一个充满矛盾的社会，他们一方面痴迷于寻找其他感性物种的念头，另一方面又拒绝接受自己并不孤单的事实。他们竭尽全力证伪所有的外星生命，他们的偏见也渗入入了科技当中。然而，通过合适的调整，我们确实可以修复他们强大的探测阵列。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

The Yuht were a paradoxical society, obsessed with the idea of finding other sentient species, yet refusing to accept that they were not alone. They were so focused on disproving any claim of alien life, that their bias found a way into their technology. However, with the proper modifications and calibrations, we could certainly fix their powerful detection arrays.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:961`

## tech_secrets_baol

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_baol&lang=en)

### 中文

巴奥有机工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

Baol Organic Plant

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:962`

## tech_secrets_baol_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_baol_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巴奥有机体制造工具的手段非常独特。由于该物种的行动相对迟缓，他们会在一种又像果园又像工厂的设施中直接利用有机物种出需要的工具。通过合适的基因调控，我们也可以在普通的农田边上种出许多物品和工具。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

The Baol Organism had a unique way of producing tools. Given the species' relative slowness, they would simply grow them out of organic matter in locations that were half orchard, half factory. With the proper genetic adjustments, we could also grow various commodities and tools alongside our regular crops.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:963`

## tech_secrets_zroni

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_zroni&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞风暴投射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

Zroni Storm Caster

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:964`

## tech_secrets_zroni_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_secrets_zroni_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞是一种令人惊叹的物质，倾向狂暴地释放能量。我们认为只要正确利用文物就可以生成覆盖一整个恒星系的宇宙风暴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Zro is a fascinating element, prone to chaotic bursts of energy. We believe that with the right artifacts, we could use it to fuel spatial storms over entire solar systems.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:965`

## tech_archaeoshield

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeoshield&lang=en)

### 中文

远古延留力场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Ancient Suspension Field

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:967`

## tech_archaeoshield_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeoshield_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

延留力场的投射距离远超普通护盾，它能在着弹瞬间利用远古文物将时间流速减缓，给予护盾更长的时间窗口来消耗来袭攻击的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Projected farther than regular shields, the suspension field technology uses ancient artifacts to slow time at the point of impact to afford the shields a longer opportunity to dispel the incoming attack's energy.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:968`

## tech_archaeoarmor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeoarmor&lang=en)

### 中文

远古脉冲装甲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

Ancient Pulse Armor

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:969`

## tech_archaeoarmor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeoarmor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用磁能脉冲和场投射原理，这种离子化的装甲板可以负载反应装药，缓解碰撞的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Ionized plates of armor can carry a reactive charge to alleviate the energy of impacts, using magnetic repulsion and field projection principles.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:970`

## tech_archaeo_detection_scrambler

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_detection_scrambler&lang=en)

### 中文

远古索敌扰频器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

Ancient Target Scrambler

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:971`

## tech_archaeo_detection_scrambler_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_detection_scrambler_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

具有正确波长的回显中继器和光扩散器可以产生破坏性干涉，有效地扰乱敌方索敌系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

Ping repeaters and optic diffusers with the right wavelengths can produce destructive interference and effectively scramble hostile targeting systems.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:972`

## TECH_UNLOCK_SCRAMBLER_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECH_UNLOCK_SCRAMBLER_TITLE&lang=en)

### English

$TECH_UNLOCK_COMPONENT_LINE$ $tech_archaeo_detection_scrambler$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:973`

## TECH_UNLOCK_SCRAMBLER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECH_UNLOCK_SCRAMBLER_DESC&lang=en)

$tech_archaeo_detection_scrambler_desc$

### English

$tech_archaeo_detection_scrambler_desc$

## tech_archaeo_rampart

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_rampart&lang=en)

### 中文

远古壁垒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:868`

### English

Ancient Rampart

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:975`

## tech_archaeo_rampart_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_rampart_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在安保方面，尽管防御平台性价比极高，但它们仍需要在控制中心的操作下运行。强化我们的恒星基地，提升其在遭受攻击时的生存能力，并最大化我们对独立轨道防御设施的组网能力将会让我们有更充裕的时间派遣增援。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

Defense platforms are a cost effective way to ensure our safety, but they need to be monitored from an operation center. Fortifying our Starbase to ensure its survivability in the event of an attack, and maximizing our capacity to network independent orbital defenses would give us more time to dispatch reinforcements.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:976`

## tech_archaeo_titan_beam

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_titan_beam&lang=en)

### 中文

远古湮灭眩光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

Ancient Ruination Glare

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:977`

## tech_archaeo_titan_beam_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_titan_beam_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

高强度中射程火力将会让我们的泰坦舰更加轻易地突破敌人的战线。这些远古的火炮曾是一个星海帝国建立的根基。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

Tremendous medium range firepower will enable our titans to break through enemy lines. These ancient cannons were once the foundation upon which a galactic empire was built.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:978`

## tech_archaeo_overcharger

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_overcharger&lang=en)

### 中文

远古护盾超频器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

Ancient Shield Overcharger

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:979`

## tech_archaeo_overcharger_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_overcharger_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于远古稳定化线圈和阻尼器，护盾可以安全地进行超频。除了产生的力场更强以外，这也可以直接汽化掉部分来袭的弹丸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

Supercharging shields can be done safely thanks to ancient stabilizer coils and dampeners. In addition to the production of a stronger force field, this also has the added benefit of outright vaporizing certain incoming projectiles.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:980`

## tech_archaeo_refinery

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_refinery&lang=en)

### 中文

远古精炼厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:874`

### English

Ancient Refinery

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:981`

## tech_archaeo_refinery_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_refinery_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种具有数百万年历史的处理设施拥有着超过100%的效率。它的运作方式依旧是个谜，但它对产出的巨大提升是难以估量的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:875`

### English

This millennia-old processing equipment has an efficiency rate above 100%. Its functioning remains alien, but its production gains are substantial.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:982`

## tech_archaeo_pk_devolving_beam

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_pk_devolving_beam&lang=en)

### 中文

降智光束

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

Devolving Beam

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:983`

## tech_archaeo_pk_devolving_beam_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_pk_devolving_beam_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种巨像级心智抹除武器，可以释放覆盖整颗行星的能量波，将大多数感性生命变回未开智状态。我们估计在进行进一步调整之后，它甚至还可以用来抹除机器单元的记忆体使其瘫痪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

A colossal mind-wiping cannon which unleashes a planet-wide wave of energy, returning most sentient life to a pre-sapient state. We estimate that with additional tuning, it might also be used to wipe clean memory banks and disable robotic units.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:984`

## is_ai_world_no_archaeoengineers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ais_ai_world_no_archaeoengineers&lang=en)

### 中文

§R$TRIGGER_FAIL$人工智能世界需要$ap_archaeoengineers$飞升天赋才能被$tech_archaeo_pk_devolving_beam$§!当成目标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:878`

### English

§R$TRIGGER_FAIL$AI Worlds require the $ap_archaeoengineers$ Ascension Perk to be targeted by the $tech_archaeo_pk_devolving_beam$§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:985`

## tech_archaeo_mass_drivers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_mass_drivers&lang=en)

### 中文

远古巨炮组

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:879`

### English

Ancient Macro Batteries

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:986`

## tech_archaeo_mass_drivers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_mass_drivers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些硕大无朋的炮组发射相对缓慢但是极具破坏力的弹丸，确切地说它们什么都能射，包括金属废弃物和小行星碎块等等。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:880`

### English

These cannons of gargantuan size deliver relatively slow but devastating projectiles, and can fire pretty much anything, from scrap metal, to asteroid chunks.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:987`

## tech_archaeo_lasers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_lasers&lang=en)

### 中文

远古穴蚀坍缩器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

Ancient Cavitation Collapser

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:988`

## tech_archaeo_lasers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_lasers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种高能武器在命中时会产生狂暴的微坍缩事件，向目标的装甲中释放巨量热量和应力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

This high energy weapon causes brutal micro collapsing events on impact, generating tremendous heat and force inside its targets armor.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:989`

## tech_archaeo_point_defence

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_point_defence&lang=en)

### 中文

远古防御投网机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

Ancient Defensive Web Slinger

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:990`

## tech_archaeo_point_defence_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_point_defence_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

要说锁定和拦截导弹这活儿，这些古怪的炮阵可是超级棒，以远古代码写就的预测算法可以织造一张滴水不漏的火力防御网。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

When it comes to tracking and shooting down missiles, these antique batteries are the best of the best, making use of a predictive algorithm written in long forgotten code to weave a fiery defensive web.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:991`

## tech_archaeo_missiles

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_missiles&lang=en)

### 中文

远古纳米导弹云发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

Ancient Nano-Missile Cloud Launcher

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:992`

## tech_archaeo_missiles_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_missiles_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当利用远古科技稳定化后，我们便可以用粒子电荷创造携带有载荷颇具威力的微导弹簇群，可用于饱和对方的点防御系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

Particle charges, once stabilized thanks to ancient technology, allow us to create swarms of tiny missiles that still carry a respectable payload, and should be able to overwhelm most point-defense systems.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:993`

## tech_archaeo_mass_accelerator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_mass_accelerator&lang=en)

### 中文

远古饱和剂发射炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

Ancient Saturator Artillery

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:994`

## tech_archaeo_mass_accelerator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_mass_accelerator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种火炮发射一系列微小的离子化球体弹幕用来中和大部分的力场，使其拥有极高的护盾穿透率。虽有理论认为这些漂亮的球体可以用作珠宝，但在一场熔毁了整间实验室的严重事故后，所有相关提案都被驳回了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

This cannon fires a barrage of ancient miniscule ionized spheres that charge upon contact with most force fields, giving them high levels of shield penetration. Any theory that these spheres could have been used for jewelry purposes were dismissed when a critical incident melted an entire laboratory.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:995`

## tech_archaeo_strike_crafts

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_strike_crafts&lang=en)

### 中文

远古钻孔机无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

Ancient Driller Drones

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:996`

## tech_archaeo_strike_crafts_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_archaeo_strike_crafts_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些高速无人机装备有独特的采矿激光，它们几乎可以无视目标的任何镀层防护。这么好的科技只用来采矿岂不是太浪费了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

These high velocity drones have been equipped with antique mining lasers that virtually ignore any plating protecting their target. A tech too good to be wasted on astro mining.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:997`

## sm_zroni_storm_caster_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_zroni_storm_caster_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

风暴投射器并不完全是泽珞族的科技。我们只是发现将泽珞暴露在极端环境下将会以一种极度混沌的形式释放其中的潜能，产生各种危险的干扰和自然现象。在一系列引人深思的事故后，我们发现了驯服这一力量的一种方式：用泽珞族制作的晶体产出一种强大的灵能风暴席卷一整个恒星系。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:918`

### English

The Storm Caster is not quite Zroni Technology. We simply realized that exposing zro to extreme conditions could cause it to release its potential energy in an erratic manner, leading to all sorts of dangerous interferences and phenomenon. After a series of insightful incidents, we have discovered a way to harness this energy through Zroni-made crystals to produce a powerful psychic storm in a solar system.\n

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:1032`

## zroni_storm_caster_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_storm_caster_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个恒星系中制造一场§Y人工风暴§!：\n阻碍§Y探测器§!\n降低§Y舰船速度§!和§Y射速§!\n无效化§Y装甲§!\n强化§Y护盾§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:920`

### English

Creates an §YArtificial Storm§! in the system:\nBlocks §YSensors§!\nReduces §YShip Speed§! and §YFire Rate§!\nNullifies §YArmor§!\nImproves §YShields§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:1034`

## improved_zroni_storm_caster_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aimproved_zroni_storm_caster_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个恒星系中制造一场§Y人工风暴§!：\n阻碍§Y探测器§!\n降低§Y舰船速度§!和§Y射速§!\n无效化§Y装甲§!\n强化§Y护盾§!\n对该恒星系内的§Y敌对舰船§!造成持续伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:921`

### English

Creates an §YArtificial Storm§! in the system:\nBlocks §YSensors§!\nReduces §YShip Speed§! and §YFire Rate§!\nNullifies §YArmor§!\nImproves §YShields§!\nConstantly damages §YHostile Ships§! in the system.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:1035`

## STARBASE_AURA_ZRONI_STORM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTARBASE_AURA_ZRONI_STORM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

控制风暴的整体极化率可以将其威力提升到对任何准备不充分的舰船而言都很危险的级别，同时又能对我们自己维持安全条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

Controlling the Storm's general polarity allows to increase its power to dangerous levels for any unprepared vessel while maintaining safe conditions for our own.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:1109`

## recently_devolved

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecently_devolved&lang=en)

£trigger_no£这颗星球最近发生了退化，残存的能量将会影响进一步的尝试。我们必须等待至少§Y1年§!£time£才能再次向其开火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

## planet_destruction.901.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_destruction.901.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着[FromFrom.GetName]发出的意识清除光束的能量逐渐消散，[From.GetName]的人口几乎都已经退化到了未开智状态。幸免遇难者面对那些曾经认识的人们做出动物般的举动只能束手无策。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_l_simp_chinese.yml:1046`

### English

As the energies of the mind wiping beam from the [FromFrom.GetName] dissipate, [From.GetName]'s population has almost entirely regressed to the pre-sapient stage. Those who were spared can now only watch in horror as those they once knew return to animalistic behaviors.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_l_english.yml:1176`
